<div id="cookie-consent" class="fixed bottom-4 left-4 z-[70] hidden w-[calc(100vw-2rem)] max-w-[360px]">
        <div class="rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 bg-white px-4 py-4 shadow-[0_18px_60px_rgba(0,0,0,0.18)]">
            <div class="flex flex-col gap-3 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
                <div class="min-w-0">
                    <p class="text-sm font-semibold text-black">Çerez Tercihleri</p>
                    <p class="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-gray-600">Size daha iyi bir deneyim sunmak, trafiği analiz etmek ve reklam alanlarını optimize etmek için çerezler kullanıyoruz.</p>
                </div>
                <div class="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2">
                    <button type="button" data-cookie-action="reject" class="rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700 transition hover:border-gray-400 hover:text-black">Sadece Zorunlu</button>
                    <button type="button" data-cookie-action="accept" class="rounded-lg bg-[#e30613] px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-[#b90a16]">Tümünü Kabul Et</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
</div>

<script>
(() => {
    const siteName = "Diye Konu\u015ftu";
    // ── Like persistence ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const initLikes = () => {
        document.querySelectorAll('.article-dislike-toggle').forEach(btn => {
            const slug = btn.dataset.articleSlug;
            if (!slug) return;
            const countNode = btn.querySelector('.article-dislike-count');
            const cnt = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('dislike-count-' + slug) || '0', 10);
            if (countNode) countNode.textContent = cnt;
            if (localStorage.getItem('dislike-state-' + slug) === '1') btn.classList.add('text-red-600', 'border-red-300', 'bg-red-50');
        });
        document.querySelectorAll('.article-like-toggle').forEach(btn => {
            const slug = btn.dataset.articleSlug;
            if (!slug) return;
            const countNode = btn.querySelector('.article-like-count');
            const htmlCount = parseInt(btn.dataset.likeCount || '0', 10);
            const stored = localStorage.getItem('like-count-' + slug);
            const displayCount = stored !== null ? parseInt(stored, 10) : htmlCount;
            if (stored === null) localStorage.setItem('like-count-' + slug, htmlCount);
            const isLiked = localStorage.getItem('like-state-' + slug) === '1';
            if (countNode) countNode.textContent = displayCount;
            btn.dataset.likeCount = displayCount;
            if (isLiked) btn.classList.add('text-[#e30613]');
        });
    };
    initLikes();

    // Dislike standalone listener
    document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        const btn = e.target.closest('.article-dislike-toggle');
        if (!btn) return;
        const slug = btn.dataset.articleSlug;
        if (!slug) return;
        const stateKey = 'dislike-state-' + slug;
        const countKey = 'dislike-count-' + slug;
        const isDis = localStorage.getItem(stateKey) === '1';
        const countNode = btn.querySelector('.article-dislike-count');
        let cnt = parseInt(localStorage.getItem(countKey) || '0', 10);
        if (isDis) {
            cnt = Math.max(0, cnt - 1);
            localStorage.setItem(stateKey, '0');
            btn.classList.remove('text-red-600', 'border-red-300', 'bg-red-50');
        } else {
            cnt = cnt + 1;
            localStorage.setItem(stateKey, '1');
            btn.classList.add('text-red-600', 'border-red-300', 'bg-red-50');
        }
        localStorage.setItem(countKey, cnt);
        if (countNode) countNode.textContent = cnt;
    });

    const streamContainer = document.querySelector('.article-stream-root');
    const initialArticleUrl = "https:\/\/diyekonustu.com\/kamera-rezaletinin-golgesinde-kongre-yeni-parti-samsunda-yarin-secim-var-kavgalar-ve-on-secimler-hafizalarda";
    const initialArticleTitle = "Kamera rezaletinin g\u00f6lgesinde kongre! Yeni Parti Samsun\u0027da yar\u0131n se\u00e7im var: Kavgalar ve \u00f6n se\u00e7imler haf\u0131zalarda";

    const loadedSlugs = new Set(["kamera-rezaletinin-golgesinde-kongre-yeni-parti-samsunda-yarin-secim-var-kavgalar-ve-on-secimler-hafizalarda"]);
    const viewedSlugs = new Set();
    let loading = false;
    let activeUtterance = null;
    let activeButton = null;
    let activeArticleUrl = initialArticleUrl;

    const loadExternalScript = (id, src) => {
        if (document.getElementById(id)) {
            return;
        }

        const script = document.createElement('script');
        script.id = id;
        script.async = true;
        script.src = src;
        document.head.appendChild(script);
    };

    const hydrateSocialEmbeds = (root = document) => {
        let needsTwitter = false;
        let needsInstagram = false;
        const scope = root.querySelectorAll ? root : document;

        scope.querySelectorAll('.article-content-body a[href]').forEach((anchor) => {
            if (anchor.closest('blockquote.twitter-tweet, blockquote.instagram-media')) {
                return;
            }

            const href = anchor.href || '';
            const isTweet = /https?:\/\/(www\.)?(twitter\.com|x\.com)\/[^/]+\/status\/\d+/i.test(href);
            const isInstagram = /https?:\/\/(www\.)?instagram\.com\/(p|reel|tv)\/[^/?#]+/i.test(href);

            if (!isTweet && !isInstagram) {
                return;
            }

            const wrapper = document.createElement('blockquote');
            const link = document.createElement('a');
            link.href = href;
            link.textContent = anchor.textContent?.trim() || href;

            if (isTweet) {
                wrapper.className = 'twitter-tweet';
                wrapper.dataset.dnt = 'true';
                wrapper.dataset.cards = 'hidden';
                wrapper.dataset.conversation = 'none';
                wrapper.dataset.theme = 'light';
                wrapper.appendChild(link);
                needsTwitter = true;
            } else {
                wrapper.className = 'instagram-media';
                wrapper.dataset.instgrmPermalink = href;
                wrapper.dataset.instgrmVersion = '14';
                wrapper.appendChild(link);
                needsInstagram = true;
            }

            const parent = anchor.parentElement;
            if (parent && parent.tagName === 'P' && parent.textContent.trim() === anchor.textContent.trim()) {
                parent.replaceWith(wrapper);
            } else {
                anchor.replaceWith(wrapper);
            }
        });

        if (needsTwitter || scope.querySelector('.twitter-tweet')) {
            loadExternalScript('twitter-widgets-js', 'https://platform.twitter.com/widgets.js');
            window.twttr?.widgets?.load(scope);
        }

        if (needsInstagram || scope.querySelector('.instagram-media')) {
            loadExternalScript('instagram-embed-js', 'https://www.instagram.com/embed.js');
            window.instgrm?.Embeds?.process();
        }
    };

    const normalizeStreamArticle = (articleNode) => {
        if (!articleNode) {
            return;
        }

        const streamShareSlot = articleNode.querySelector('[data-stream-meta-share-slot]');
        const streamShareMenu = articleNode.querySelector('[data-stream-share-menu]');
        if (streamShareSlot && streamShareMenu && !streamShareSlot.contains(streamShareMenu)) {
            streamShareSlot.appendChild(streamShareMenu);
        }

        articleNode.querySelectorAll('.cover-share-dropdown').forEach((dropdown) => {
            dropdown.hidden = false;
            dropdown.style.removeProperty('display');
        });

        articleNode.querySelectorAll('.cover-share-menu').forEach((menu) => {
            menu.classList.remove('is-open', 'open', 'active', 'show');
        });

        articleNode.querySelectorAll('.article-cover-frame, .article-cover-image, .article-content-body img, .article-content-body iframe, .article-content-body video').forEach((node) => {
            node.style.maxWidth = '100%';
            node.style.width = '100%';
            node.style.marginLeft = '0';
            node.style.marginRight = '0';
            node.style.transform = 'none';
            node.style.borderRadius = '0';
        });

        articleNode.querySelectorAll('.article-content-body iframe, .article-content-body video').forEach((node) => {
            node.style.display = 'block';
        });
    };

    const trackPageView = (slug, url, title) => {
        if (!slug || viewedSlugs.has(slug)) {
            return;
        }

        viewedSlugs.add(slug);

        fetch("https:\/\/diyekonustu.com\/api\/article-view\/__SLUG__".replace('__SLUG__', encodeURIComponent(slug)), {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                'X-CSRF-TOKEN': document.querySelector('meta[name=\"csrf-token\"]')?.content || '',
                'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',
                'Accept': 'application/json',
            },
            credentials: 'same-origin',
            body: JSON.stringify({ source: 'infinity' }),
        }).catch(() => {});

        if (typeof window.gtag === 'function') {
            window.gtag('event', 'page_view', {
                page_title: title,
                page_location: url,
                page_path: new URL(url, window.location.origin).pathname,
            });
        }

        if (Array.isArray(window.dataLayer)) {
            window.dataLayer.push({
                event: 'virtual_page_view',
                page_title: title,
                page_location: url,
                page_path: new URL(url, window.location.origin).pathname,
            });
        }
    };

    const setActiveArticle = (articleNode) => {
        if (!articleNode) {
            return;
        }

        const nextSlug = articleNode.dataset.articleSlug || '';
        const nextUrl = articleNode.dataset.articleUrl || initialArticleUrl;
        const nextTitle = articleNode.dataset.articleTitle || initialArticleTitle;
        const historyUrl = new URL(nextUrl, window.location.origin);
        const historyPath = `${historyUrl.pathname}${historyUrl.search}${historyUrl.hash}`;

        if (!nextUrl || activeArticleUrl === historyPath) {
            return;
        }

        history.replaceState({ articleSlug: nextSlug }, '', historyPath);
        document.title = `${nextTitle} - ${siteName}`;
        activeArticleUrl = historyPath;
        trackPageView(nextSlug, historyUrl.href, nextTitle);
    };

    const stopAudio = () => {
        clearTicker();
        window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
        if (activeButton) {
            setAudioActive(activeButton, false);
            updateWidgetUI(activeButton, 0, activeTotalSec);
        }
        activeUtterance  = null;
        activeButton     = null;
        activeElapsedSec = 0;
    };

    const observeSentinels = () => {
        const sentinels = streamContainer.querySelectorAll('.article-stream-sentinel[data-next-slug]');

        sentinels.forEach((sentinel) => {
            if (sentinel.dataset.bound === 'true') {
                return;
            }

            sentinel.dataset.bound = 'true';
            observer.observe(sentinel);
        });
    };

    const observeArticleBlocks = () => {
        const articleBlocks = streamContainer.querySelectorAll('.stream-article-block');

        articleBlocks.forEach((block) => {
            if (block.dataset.articleObserved === 'true') {
                return;
            }

            block.dataset.articleObserved = 'true';
            articleViewObserver.observe(block);
        });
    };

    const loadNextArticle = async (sentinel) => {
        const nextSlug = sentinel.dataset.nextSlug;

        if (!nextSlug || loading || loadedSlugs.has(nextSlug)) {
            sentinel.removeAttribute('data-next-slug');
            return;
        }

        loading = true;
        sentinel.textContent = 'Haber yükleniyor';

        try {
            const streamUrlTemplate = "https:\/\/diyekonustu.com\/__SLUG__\/stream";
            const response = await fetch(streamUrlTemplate.replace('__SLUG__', encodeURIComponent(nextSlug)), {
                headers: {
                    'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',
                    'Accept': 'application/json',
                },
            });

            if (!response.ok) {
                throw new Error('Stream fetch failed');
            }

            const data = await response.json();
            const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
            wrapper.innerHTML = data.html;

            const articleNode = wrapper.firstElementChild;

            if (articleNode) {
                loadedSlugs.add(nextSlug);
                normalizeStreamArticle(articleNode);
                sentinel.replaceWith(articleNode);
                hydrateSocialEmbeds(articleNode);
                window.initCoverArticleListen?.(articleNode);
                observeSentinels();
                observeArticleBlocks();
                window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                    syncActiveArticleOnScroll();
                });
            } else {
                sentinel.textContent = 'Yeni haber bulunamadı';
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(error);
            sentinel.textContent = 'Yeni haber yüklenemedi';
        } finally {
            loading = false;
        }
    };

    const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
        entries.forEach((entry) => {
            if (!entry.isIntersecting) {
                return;
            }

            observer.unobserve(entry.target);
            loadNextArticle(entry.target);
        });
    }, {
        rootMargin: '600px 0px',
    });

    const articleViewObserver = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
        let topEntry = null;

        entries.forEach((entry) => {
            if (!entry.isIntersecting) {
                return;
            }

            if (!topEntry || entry.intersectionRatio > topEntry.intersectionRatio) {
                topEntry = entry;
            }
        });

        if (topEntry?.target) {
            setActiveArticle(topEntry.target);
        }
    }, {
        threshold: [0.35, 0.6, 0.85],
        rootMargin: '-10% 0px -35% 0px',
    });

    observeArticleBlocks();
    const initialBlock = streamContainer.querySelector('.stream-article-block');
    hydrateSocialEmbeds(streamContainer || document);
    setActiveArticle(initialBlock);
    if (initialBlock?.dataset.articleSlug) {
        viewedSlugs.add(initialBlock.dataset.articleSlug);
    }

    let scrollTicking = false;
    const syncActiveArticleOnScroll = () => {
        if (scrollTicking) {
            return;
        }

        scrollTicking = true;

        window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
            const blocks = [...streamContainer.querySelectorAll('.stream-article-block')];
            let currentBlock = blocks[0] || null;
            let bestDistance = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;

            blocks.forEach((block) => {
                const rect = block.getBoundingClientRect();
                const distance = Math.abs(rect.top - 120);

                if (rect.bottom > 160 && distance < bestDistance) {
                    bestDistance = distance;
                    currentBlock = block;
                }
            });

            if (currentBlock) {
                setActiveArticle(currentBlock);
            }

            scrollTicking = false;
        });
    };

    window.addEventListener('scroll', syncActiveArticleOnScroll, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('resize', syncActiveArticleOnScroll, { passive: true });

    // ── Helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const fmtTime = (s) => {
        s = Math.max(0, Math.floor(s));
        return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    };

    const getWidget = (btn) => btn.closest('.article-audio-widget');

    const setAudioActive = (btn, active) => {
        const pi = btn.querySelector('.article-audio-play-icon');
        const si = btn.querySelector('.article-audio-stop-icon');
        if (active) {
            btn.classList.add('bg-[#e30613]', 'text-white', 'border-[#e30613]');
            btn.classList.remove('bg-white', 'border-gray-800');
            pi?.classList.add('hidden'); si?.classList.remove('hidden');
        } else {
            btn.classList.remove('bg-[#e30613]', 'text-white', 'border-[#e30613]');
            btn.classList.add('bg-white', 'border-gray-800');
            pi?.classList.remove('hidden'); si?.classList.add('hidden');
        }
    };

    const updateWidgetUI = (btn, elapsedSec, totalSec) => {
        const w = getWidget(btn);
        if (!w) return;
        const pct = totalSec > 0 ? Math.min((elapsedSec / totalSec) * 100, 100) : 0;
        w.querySelector('.article-audio-progress-bar').style.width = pct + '%';
        w.querySelector('.article-audio-elapsed').textContent  = fmtTime(elapsedSec);
        w.querySelector('.article-audio-remaining').textContent = fmtTime(totalSec - elapsedSec);
    };

    // ── Audio state ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    let activePlainText  = '';
    let activeRate       = 1;
    let activeTotalSec   = 0;
    let activeElapsedSec = 0;
    let activeTicker     = null;

    const CHARS_PER_SEC = 14; // ~14 chars/sec at rate 1.0

    const clearTicker = () => { if (activeTicker) { clearInterval(activeTicker); activeTicker = null; } };

    const doSpeak = (btn, fromSec) => {
        const charOffset = Math.max(0, Math.floor(fromSec * CHARS_PER_SEC * activeRate));
        const chunk = activePlainText.slice(charOffset);
        if (!chunk.trim()) { stopAudio(); return; }

        const utt = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(chunk);
        utt.lang = 'tr-TR';
        utt.rate = activeRate;
        utt.pitch = 1;

        const voices = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
        const tr = voices.filter(v => v.lang?.toLowerCase().startsWith('tr'));
        const hints = ['female','woman','zira','seda','selin','ceren'];
        const voice = tr.find(v => hints.some(h => v.name.toLowerCase().includes(h))) ?? tr[0] ?? voices[0];
        if (voice) utt.voice = voice;

        activeButton = btn;
        activeUtterance = utt;
        setAudioActive(btn, true);

        clearTicker();
        activeTicker = setInterval(() => {
            activeElapsedSec = Math.min(activeElapsedSec + 0.25, activeTotalSec);
            updateWidgetUI(btn, activeElapsedSec, activeTotalSec);
            if (activeElapsedSec >= activeTotalSec) { clearTicker(); stopAudio(); }
        }, 250);

        utt.onend   = () => { clearTicker(); if (activeButton) stopAudio(); };
        utt.onerror = () => { clearTicker(); if (activeButton) stopAudio(); };

        window.speechSynthesis.speak(utt);
    };

    const seekAndSpeak = (btn, newElapsedSec) => {
        clearTicker();
        const targetSec  = Math.max(0, Math.min(newElapsedSec, activeTotalSec - 1));
        const savedText  = activePlainText;
        const savedTotal = activeTotalSec;
        const savedRate  = activeRate;
        activeButton = null; // prevent onend/onerror from calling full stopAudio reset
        window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
        // restore state after cancel
        activePlainText  = savedText;
        activeTotalSec   = savedTotal;
        activeRate       = savedRate;
        activeElapsedSec = targetSec;
        setTimeout(() => doSpeak(btn, targetSec), 150);
    };


    const fallbackCopy = (text, cb) => {
        const ta = document.createElement('textarea');
        ta.value = text;
        ta.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;opacity:0;top:0;left:0;';
        document.body.appendChild(ta);
        ta.focus(); ta.select();
        try { document.execCommand('copy'); cb(); } catch(e) {}
        document.body.removeChild(ta);
    };

        // ── Click delegation ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {

        // iOS'ta Facebook'un yeni sekme yönlendirmesi bazen ana sayfaya düşebiliyor.
        // Mobil paylaşım uç noktasını aynı sekmede açarak doğrudan paylaşım ekranına gidiyoruz.
        const facebookShareLink = event.target.closest('[data-facebook-share]');
        if (facebookShareLink && window.matchMedia('(max-width: 768px)').matches) {
            event.preventDefault();
            window.location.assign(facebookShareLink.dataset.facebookMobileUrl || facebookShareLink.href);
            return;
        }

        // Copy link
        const copyBtn = event.target.closest('.share-copy-link');
        if (copyBtn) {
            const url = copyBtn.dataset.shareUrl ?? '';
            const lbl = copyBtn.querySelector('.share-copy-label');
            const success = () => { if (lbl) { lbl.textContent = 'Kopyalandı!'; setTimeout(() => lbl.textContent = 'Kopyala', 2000); } };
            if (navigator.clipboard && window.isSecureContext) {
                navigator.clipboard.writeText(url).then(success).catch(() => fallbackCopy(url, success));
            } else {
                fallbackCopy(url, success);
            }
            return;
        }

        // Like
        const likeBtn = event.target.closest('.article-like-toggle');
        if (likeBtn) {
            const slug = likeBtn.dataset.articleSlug;
            const countNode = likeBtn.querySelector('.article-like-count');
            if (!slug || !countNode) return;
            const stateKey = `like-state-${slug}`;
            const countKey = `like-count-${slug}`;
            const isLiked = localStorage.getItem(stateKey) === '1';
            let n = parseInt(localStorage.getItem(countKey) ?? '0', 10);
            if (isLiked) {
                localStorage.setItem(stateKey, '0');
                n = Math.max(0, n - 1);
                likeBtn.classList.remove('text-[#e30613]');
            } else {
                localStorage.setItem(stateKey, '1');
                n += 1;
                likeBtn.classList.add('text-[#e30613]');
            }
            localStorage.setItem(countKey, n);
            likeBtn.dataset.likeCount = n;
            countNode.textContent = n;
            return;
        }


        // Audio play/pause
        const audioBtn = event.target.closest('.article-audio-toggle');
        if (audioBtn) {
            if (activeButton === audioBtn) { stopAudio(); return; }
            stopAudio();

            const articleEl = audioBtn.closest('article') ?? document;
            const body    = articleEl.querySelector('.article-content-body')?.textContent?.trim() ?? '';
            const title   = articleEl.querySelector('h1, h2')?.textContent?.trim() ?? '';
            const summary = articleEl.querySelector('.article-summary')?.textContent?.trim() ?? '';
            if (!body) return;

            activePlainText  = [title, summary, body].filter(Boolean).join('. ');
            activeTotalSec   = Math.ceil(activePlainText.length / (CHARS_PER_SEC * activeRate));
            activeElapsedSec = 0;

            updateWidgetUI(audioBtn, 0, activeTotalSec);
            doSpeak(audioBtn, 0);
            return;
        }

        // -10s / +10s
        const skipTarget = event.target.closest('[data-audio-action]');
        if (skipTarget && activeButton) {
            const action = skipTarget.dataset.audioAction;
            if (action === 'back') { seekAndSpeak(activeButton, activeElapsedSec - 10); return; }
            if (action === 'fwd')  { seekAndSpeak(activeButton, activeElapsedSec + 10); return; }
        }

        // Speed
        const speedBtn = event.target.closest('.article-audio-speed');
        if (speedBtn) {
            const rates = [1, 1.25, 1.5, 2];
            const cur  = parseFloat(speedBtn.dataset.rate ?? '1');
            const next = rates[(rates.indexOf(cur) + 1) % rates.length];
            speedBtn.dataset.rate = next;
            speedBtn.textContent  = next + 'x';
            activeRate = next;
            if (activeButton) {
                const savedBtn  = activeButton;
                const savedText = activePlainText;
                clearTicker();
                activeButton = null;
                window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
                activePlainText  = savedText;
                activeTotalSec   = Math.ceil(savedText.length / (CHARS_PER_SEC * next));
                activeElapsedSec = 0;
                setTimeout(() => doSpeak(savedBtn, 0), 150);
            }
            return;
        }
    });

    if (streamContainer) observeSentinels();

    // Sayfadan ayrılınca sesi durdur
    const stopOnLeave = () => { if (activeButton) stopAudio(); };
    window.addEventListener('beforeunload', stopOnLeave);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => { if (document.hidden && activeButton) stopAudio(); });
})();
</script>
                <script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        const articleShareMenu = document.getElementById('article-share-menu');
        const articleMetaShareSlot = document.getElementById('article-meta-share-slot');
        if (articleShareMenu && articleMetaShareSlot && !articleMetaShareSlot.contains(articleShareMenu)) {
            articleMetaShareSlot.appendChild(articleShareMenu);
        }

        const button = document.getElementById('articleScrollTopBtn');
        if (!button) return;

        const syncVisibility = () => {
            button.classList.toggle('is-visible', window.scrollY > 480);
        };

        window.addEventListener('scroll', syncVisibility, { passive: true });
        syncVisibility();

        button.addEventListener('click', () => {
            window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
        });

        const gallerySlides = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-gallery-slide]')];
        if (gallerySlides.length > 0) {
            const maxGalleryIndex = gallerySlides.reduce((max, slide) => Math.max(max, Number(slide.dataset.galleryIndex) || 1), 1);
            let activeGalleryIndex = Math.max(1, Math.min(Number(new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('sayfa')) || 1, maxGalleryIndex));
            let suppressGalleryObserver = false;

            const galleryUrl = (index) => {
                const url = new URL(window.location.href);
                if (index > 1) {
                    url.searchParams.set('sayfa', index);
                } else {
                    url.searchParams.delete('sayfa');
                }
                return `${url.pathname}${url.search}${url.hash}`;
            };

            const trackGalleryPage = (index) => {
                if (typeof window.gtag === 'function') {
                    window.gtag('event', 'page_view', {
                        page_path: galleryUrl(index),
                        page_location: `${window.location.origin}${galleryUrl(index)}`,
                        page_title: document.title,
                    });
                }
            };

            const setGalleryHistory = (index, mode = 'push') => {
                const state = { galleryPage: index };
                const url = galleryUrl(index);

                if (mode === 'replace') {
                    history.replaceState(state, '', url);
                    return;
                }

                if (index === activeGalleryIndex && window.location.pathname + window.location.search + window.location.hash === url) {
                    return;
                }

                history.pushState(state, '', url);
                trackGalleryPage(index);
                activeGalleryIndex = index;
            };

            const scrollToGallerySlide = (index, behavior = 'smooth') => {
                const target = document.querySelector(`[data-gallery-index="${index}"]`);
                if (!target) return;

                suppressGalleryObserver = true;
                target.scrollIntoView({ block: 'start', behavior });
                window.setTimeout(() => {
                    suppressGalleryObserver = false;
                }, behavior === 'smooth' ? 700 : 120);
            };

            const activateGallerySlide = (index) => {
                index = Math.max(1, Math.min(Number(index) || 1, maxGalleryIndex));
                setGalleryHistory(index, 'push');
            };

            const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
                if (suppressGalleryObserver) return;

                const visible = entries
                    .filter((entry) => entry.isIntersecting)
                    .sort((a, b) => b.intersectionRatio - a.intersectionRatio)[0];

                if (!visible) return;
                activateGallerySlide(Number(visible.target.dataset.galleryIndex));
            }, {
                threshold: [0.35, 0.6, 0.9],
                rootMargin: '-12% 0px -35% 0px',
            });

            gallerySlides.forEach((slide) => observer.observe(slide));

            const seedInitialGalleryHistory = () => {
                const initialIndex = activeGalleryIndex;
                history.replaceState({ galleryPage: 1 }, '', galleryUrl(1));

                if (initialIndex > 1) {
                    for (let index = 2; index <= initialIndex; index++) {
                        history.pushState({ galleryPage: index }, '', galleryUrl(index));
                    }
                    activeGalleryIndex = initialIndex;
                    window.setTimeout(() => scrollToGallerySlide(initialIndex, 'auto'), 80);
                    return;
                }

                activeGalleryIndex = 1;
            };

            seedInitialGalleryHistory();

            window.addEventListener('popstate', () => {
                const pageValue = Math.max(1, Math.min(Number(new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('sayfa')) || 1, maxGalleryIndex));
                activeGalleryIndex = pageValue;
                scrollToGallerySlide(pageValue, 'auto');
            });

            window.addEventListener('pageshow', () => {
                const pageValue = Math.max(1, Math.min(Number(new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('sayfa')) || 1, maxGalleryIndex));
                if (pageValue !== activeGalleryIndex) {
                    activeGalleryIndex = pageValue;
                    scrollToGallerySlide(pageValue, 'auto');
                }
            });
        }

        const preferredArticleVoice = () => {
            const voices = window.speechSynthesis?.getVoices?.() || [];
            const trVoices = voices.filter((voice) => (voice.lang || '').toLowerCase().startsWith('tr'));
            const preferredHints = ['google türkçe', 'google turkish', 'ahmet', 'tolga', 'cem', 'male', 'erkek'];
            const avoidHints = ['female', 'woman', 'kadın', 'kadin', 'yelda'];

            return trVoices.find((voice) => preferredHints.some((hint) => voice.name.toLowerCase().includes(hint)))
                || trVoices.find((voice) => !avoidHints.some((hint) => voice.name.toLowerCase().includes(hint)))
                || trVoices[0]
                || voices[0]
                || null;
        };

        const initCoverArticleListen = (root = document) => {
            if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) return;

            root.querySelectorAll('[data-article-listen]').forEach((listenButton) => {
                if (listenButton.dataset.listenBound === 'true') return;
                listenButton.dataset.listenBound = 'true';

            const elapsedEl = listenButton.querySelector('[data-listen-elapsed]');
            const remainingEl = listenButton.querySelector('[data-listen-remaining]');
            const fillEl = listenButton.querySelector('[data-listen-fill]');
            const dotEl = listenButton.querySelector('[data-listen-dot]');
            const backButton = listenButton.querySelector('[data-listen-back]');
            const forwardButton = listenButton.querySelector('[data-listen-forward]');
            const articleScope = listenButton.closest('.stream-article-block') || listenButton.closest('.article-page-root') || document;
            const titleText = articleScope.querySelector('header h1, header h2')?.textContent || document.querySelector('header h1')?.textContent || '';
            const summaryText = articleScope.querySelector('.article-summary')?.textContent || '';
            const bodyText = articleScope.querySelector('.article-content-body')?.textContent || '';
            const readText = [titleText, summaryText, bodyText]
                .join('. ')
                .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
                .trim();

            let utterance = null;
            let startedAt = 0;
            let pausedAt = 0;
            let elapsedBeforePause = 0;
            let timer = null;
            let pendingSpeakTimer = null;
            let speakRequestId = 0;
            const estimatedDuration = Math.max(30, Math.ceil(readText.length / 13));

            const formatTime = (seconds) => {
                const safeSeconds = Math.max(0, Math.round(seconds));
                const mins = Math.floor(safeSeconds / 60);
                const secs = String(safeSeconds % 60).padStart(2, '0');
                return `${mins}:${secs}`;
            };

            const paintProgress = (elapsed = 0) => {
                const safeElapsed = Math.min(estimatedDuration, Math.max(0, elapsed));
                const percent = estimatedDuration > 0 ? (safeElapsed / estimatedDuration) * 100 : 0;
                if (elapsedEl) elapsedEl.textContent = formatTime(safeElapsed);
                if (remainingEl) remainingEl.textContent = `-${formatTime(estimatedDuration - safeElapsed)}`;
                if (fillEl) fillEl.style.width = `${percent}%`;
                if (dotEl) dotEl.style.left = `${percent}%`;
            };

            const stopTimer = () => {
                if (timer) {
                    window.clearInterval(timer);
                    timer = null;
                }
            };

            const stopPendingSpeak = () => {
                if (pendingSpeakTimer) {
                    window.clearTimeout(pendingSpeakTimer);
                    pendingSpeakTimer = null;
                }
            };

            const startTimer = () => {
                stopTimer();
                startedAt = Date.now();
                timer = window.setInterval(() => {
                    const elapsed = elapsedBeforePause + ((Date.now() - startedAt) / 1000);
                    paintProgress(elapsed);
                }, 250);
            };

            const resetReader = () => {
                stopTimer();
                stopPendingSpeak();
                listenButton.classList.remove('is-reading');
                elapsedBeforePause = 0;
                pausedAt = 0;
                utterance = null;
                paintProgress(0);
            };

            paintProgress(0);

            const textFromElapsed = (elapsed) => {
                if (elapsed <= 0) {
                    return readText;
                }
                const ratio = estimatedDuration > 0 ? Math.min(1, Math.max(0, elapsed / estimatedDuration)) : 0;
                const index = Math.floor(readText.length * ratio);
                const boundary = readText.indexOf(' ', Math.max(0, index - 20));
                return readText.slice(boundary > -1 ? boundary : index).trim() || readText;
            };

            const speakFrom = (elapsed, shouldPlay = true) => {
                const requestId = ++speakRequestId;
                stopPendingSpeak();
                speechSynthesis.cancel();
                stopTimer();
                elapsedBeforePause = Math.min(estimatedDuration, Math.max(0, elapsed));
                utterance = null;
                paintProgress(elapsedBeforePause);

                if (!shouldPlay) {
                    listenButton.classList.remove('is-reading');
                    return;
                }

                listenButton.classList.add('is-reading');
                pendingSpeakTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
                    if (requestId !== speakRequestId) return;

                    utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(textFromElapsed(elapsedBeforePause));
                    utterance.lang = 'tr-TR';
                    utterance.rate = 0.96;
                    utterance.pitch = 0.92;
                    const voice = preferredArticleVoice();
                    if (voice) utterance.voice = voice;
                    utterance.onend = () => {
                        if (requestId === speakRequestId) resetReader();
                    };
                    utterance.onerror = () => {
                        if (requestId === speakRequestId) resetReader();
                    };
                    speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
                    startTimer();
                }, 80);
            };

            const currentElapsed = () => {
                if (listenButton.classList.contains('is-reading') && startedAt) {
                    return elapsedBeforePause + ((Date.now() - startedAt) / 1000);
                }
                return elapsedBeforePause;
            };

            const seekBy = (seconds) => {
                const wasPlaying = listenButton.classList.contains('is-reading') && !speechSynthesis.paused;
                const nextElapsed = Math.min(estimatedDuration, Math.max(0, currentElapsed() + seconds));
                speakFrom(nextElapsed, wasPlaying);
            };

            backButton?.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
                event.preventDefault();
                event.stopPropagation();
                seekBy(-15);
            });

            forwardButton?.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
                event.preventDefault();
                event.stopPropagation();
                seekBy(15);
            });

            listenButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
                if (!readText) return;

                if (speechSynthesis.speaking && !speechSynthesis.paused) {
                    elapsedBeforePause += (Date.now() - startedAt) / 1000;
                    pausedAt = Date.now();
                    stopPendingSpeak();
                    speechSynthesis.pause();
                    stopTimer();
                    listenButton.classList.remove('is-reading');
                    return;
                }

                if (speechSynthesis.paused && utterance) {
                    speechSynthesis.resume();
                    listenButton.classList.add('is-reading');
                    startTimer();
                    return;
                }

                speakFrom(0, true);
            });
            });
        };

        window.initCoverArticleListen = initCoverArticleListen;
        initCoverArticleListen(document);
        window.speechSynthesis?.addEventListener?.('voiceschanged', () => initCoverArticleListen(document));
    });
    </script>
    <script>
    (() => {
        const selector = '.odt-article-body video, .article-cover-video';
        return;

        const primeFirstFrame = (video) => {
            const duration = Number(video.duration);

            if (!Number.isFinite(duration) || duration <= 0.1) return;

            // Sifirinci kare bazi MP4'lerde siyah olabiliyor. Ilk goruntulu kareye cok
            // kucuk bir seek ile giderek ziyaretciye bos bir video kutusu gostermiyoruz.
            const previewAt = Math.min(0.08, Math.max(0.01, duration * 0.001));

            try {
                if (Math.abs(video.currentTime - previewAt) > 0.02) {
                    video.currentTime = previewAt;
                }
            } catch (_) {
                // Tarayici henuz seek kabul etmiyorsa loadeddata sonrasi tekrar denenecek.
            }
        };

        const enhanceVideo = (video) => {
            if (!(video instanceof HTMLVideoElement) || video.dataset.dkVideoUi === 'true') return;
            video.dataset.dkVideoUi = 'true';

            const existingFigure = video.closest('figure');
            let player = existingFigure;

            if (!player) {
                player = document.createElement('div');
                player.className = 'dk-inline-video';
                video.before(player);
                player.append(video);
            } else {
                player.classList.add('dk-inline-video');
            }

            const playButton = document.createElement('button');
            playButton.type = 'button';
            playButton.className = 'dk-video-start';
            playButton.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Videoyu oynat');
            playButton.innerHTML = '<span aria-hidden="true"></span>';
            player.append(playButton);

            video.preload = 'auto';
            video.setAttribute('controls', 'controls');

            const startPlayback = () => {
                player.classList.add('is-playing');
                video.setAttribute('controls', 'controls');

                const playback = video.play();
                playback?.catch(() => {
                    player.classList.remove('is-playing');
                    video.setAttribute('controls', 'controls');
                });
            };

            playButton.addEventListener('click', startPlayback);
            video.addEventListener('play', () => player.classList.add('is-playing'));
            video.addEventListener('ended', () => {
                player.classList.remove('is-playing');
                video.setAttribute('controls', 'controls');
                primeFirstFrame(video);
            });
            video.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', () => primeFirstFrame(video));
            video.addEventListener('loadeddata', () => {
                primeFirstFrame(video);
                window.requestAnimationFrame(() => primeFirstFrame(video));
            });

            const xPostSource = String(video.dataset.xCoverVideoSource || '').trim();
            if (xPostSource !== '') {
                fetch('/api/x-video?url=' + encodeURIComponent(xPostSource), { headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } })
                    .then((response) => response.ok ? response.json() : Promise.reject(new Error('X video çözümleme hatası')))
                    .then((payload) => {
                        const rawUrl = String(payload?.video_url || '').trim();
                        if (!rawUrl) throw new Error('X video adresi bulunamadı');

                        video.src = /^https?:\/\/video\.twimg\.com\//i.test(rawUrl)
                            ? '/api/x-video-proxy?url=' + encodeURIComponent(rawUrl)
                            : rawUrl;

                        if (payload?.thumbnail) video.poster = payload.thumbnail;
                        video.load();
                    })
                    .catch(() => {
                        video.closest('.article-cover-video-frame')?.classList.add('has-video-error');
                    });
            } else if (video.readyState >= HTMLMediaElement.HAVE_METADATA) {
                primeFirstFrame(video);
            } else {
                video.load();
            }
        };

        const enhanceVideos = (root = document) => {
            if (root instanceof Element && root.matches(selector)) {
                enhanceVideo(root);
            }

            root.querySelectorAll?.(selector).forEach(enhanceVideo);
        };

        enhanceVideos(document);

        new MutationObserver((records) => {
            records.forEach((record) => record.addedNodes.forEach((node) => {
                if (node.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE) {
                    enhanceVideos(node);
                }
            }));
        }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    })();
    </script>
    <script>
    (() => {
        const arrangeArticleServices = () => {
            const mobile = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 899px)').matches;
            document.querySelectorAll('.article-page-root').forEach((root) => {
                const panel = root.querySelector('.article-services-panel');
                const social = root.querySelector('.article-instagram-follow-section');
                const tags = root.querySelector('.article-tags-block');
                const latest = root.querySelector('section[aria-label="Son haberler"]');
                const priority = root.querySelector('.article-priority-actions');
                const breakingFeature = priority?.querySelector('.article-breaking-feature');
                const cover = root.querySelector('.article-cover-frame');

                // Mobilde acil aksiyonlar, kapak görselinin hemen altındadır.
                // Masaüstü düzeninde kendi sağ sütunundaki özgün yerine geri döner.
                if (priority && cover) {
                    let priorityPlaceholder = root.querySelector('[data-article-priority-placeholder]');
                    if (mobile) {
                        if (!priorityPlaceholder) {
                            priorityPlaceholder = document.createElement('div');
                            priorityPlaceholder.hidden = true;
                            priorityPlaceholder.setAttribute('data-article-priority-placeholder', '');
                            priority.parentNode.insertBefore(priorityPlaceholder, priority);
                        }
                        if (cover.nextElementSibling !== priority) {
                            cover.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', priority);
                        }
                    } else if (priorityPlaceholder) {
                        priorityPlaceholder.parentNode.insertBefore(priority, priorityPlaceholder.nextSibling);
                        priorityPlaceholder.remove();
                    }
                }

                if (!panel || !latest) return;

                let panelPlaceholder = root.querySelector('[data-article-services-placeholder]');
                let socialPlaceholder = root.querySelector('[data-article-social-placeholder]');
                let tagsPlaceholder = root.querySelector('[data-article-tags-placeholder]');
                if (mobile) {
                    if (!panelPlaceholder) {
                        panelPlaceholder = document.createElement('div');
                        panelPlaceholder.hidden = true;
                        panelPlaceholder.setAttribute('data-article-services-placeholder', '');
                        panel.parentNode.insertBefore(panelPlaceholder, panel);
                    }
                    if (social && !socialPlaceholder) {
                        socialPlaceholder = document.createElement('div');
                        socialPlaceholder.hidden = true;
                        socialPlaceholder.setAttribute('data-article-social-placeholder', '');
                        social.parentNode.insertBefore(socialPlaceholder, social);
                    }
                    if (tags && !tagsPlaceholder) {
                        tagsPlaceholder = document.createElement('div');
                        tagsPlaceholder.hidden = true;
                        tagsPlaceholder.setAttribute('data-article-tags-placeholder', '');
                        tags.parentNode.insertBefore(tagsPlaceholder, tags);
                    }
                    if (tags) latest.parentNode.insertBefore(tags, latest);
                    if (breakingFeature) {
                        let breakingPlaceholder = priority.querySelector('[data-article-breaking-placeholder]');
                        if (!breakingPlaceholder) {
                            breakingPlaceholder = document.createElement('span');
                            breakingPlaceholder.hidden = true;
                            breakingPlaceholder.setAttribute('data-article-breaking-placeholder', '');
                            priority.insertBefore(breakingPlaceholder, breakingFeature);
                        }
                    }
                    if (tags && breakingFeature) {
                        tags.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', breakingFeature);
                    } else if (breakingFeature) {
                        latest.parentNode.insertBefore(breakingFeature, latest);
                    }
                    latest.parentNode.insertBefore(panel, latest);
                    if (social) latest.parentNode.insertBefore(social, latest);
                } else {
                    const breakingPlaceholder = priority?.querySelector('[data-article-breaking-placeholder]');
                    if (breakingFeature && breakingPlaceholder) {
                        breakingPlaceholder.parentNode.insertBefore(breakingFeature, breakingPlaceholder.nextSibling);
                        breakingPlaceholder.remove();
                    }
                    if (tagsPlaceholder) {
                        tagsPlaceholder.parentNode.insertBefore(tags, tagsPlaceholder.nextSibling);
                        tagsPlaceholder.remove();
                    }
                    if (socialPlaceholder) {
                        socialPlaceholder.parentNode.insertBefore(social, socialPlaceholder.nextSibling);
                        socialPlaceholder.remove();
                    }
                    if (panelPlaceholder) {
                        panelPlaceholder.parentNode.insertBefore(panel, panelPlaceholder.nextSibling);
                        panelPlaceholder.remove();
                    }
                }
            });
        };

        // Bu script, ilgili kartların tamamından sonra parse edilir. Yerleşimi ilk
        // boyamadan önce kurmak; mobilde kartların sonradan yukarı taşınmasından
        // kaynaklanan görünür sayfa sıçramasını önler.
        arrangeArticleServices();
        document.querySelectorAll('.article-page-root.article-layout-pending').forEach((root) => {
            root.classList.remove('article-layout-pending');
            root.classList.add('article-layout-ready');
        });
        window.addEventListener('resize', arrangeArticleServices, { passive: true });
        const articleStreamRoot = document.querySelector('.article-stream-root');
        if (articleStreamRoot) {
            new MutationObserver((records) => {
                const hasNewArticle = records.some((record) => [...record.addedNodes].some((node) =>
                    node instanceof Element && (node.matches('.article-page-root') || node.querySelector('.article-page-root'))
                ));
                if (hasNewArticle) arrangeArticleServices();
            }).observe(articleStreamRoot, { childList: true, subtree: true });
        }
    })();
    </script>

<script>
(() => {
    const loadScript = (id, src, attrs = {}) => {
        if (!src || document.getElementById(id)) return null;
        const script = document.createElement('script');
        script.id = id;
        script.async = true;
        Object.entries(attrs).forEach(([key, value]) => {
            if (value !== null && value !== undefined && value !== '') {
                script.setAttribute(key, value);
            }
        });
        script.src = src;
        document.head.appendChild(script);
        return script;
    };

    const cfg = window.dkThirdPartyConfig || {};

    if (!window.dkAnalyticsBooted) {
        if (cfg.gtm) {
            window.dataLayer.push({'gtm.start': new Date().getTime(), event: 'gtm.js'});
            loadScript('dk-gtm', `https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtm.js?id=${encodeURIComponent(cfg.gtm)}`);
            window.dkAnalyticsBooted = true;
        } else if (cfg.ga) {
            loadScript('dk-gtag', `https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=${encodeURIComponent(cfg.ga)}`);
            gtag('js', new Date());
            gtag('config', cfg.ga);
            window.dkAnalyticsBooted = true;
        }
    }

    const scheduleThirdParty = () => {
        let loaded = false;
        const run = () => {
            if (loaded) return;
            loaded = true;

            if (cfg.adsense) {
                loadScript('dk-adsense', `https://pagead2.googlesyndication.com/pagead/js/adsbygoogle.js?client=${encodeURIComponent(cfg.adsense)}`, {
                    crossorigin: 'anonymous',
                });
            }

        };

        const idleRun = () => ('requestIdleCallback' in window)
            ? window.requestIdleCallback(run, { timeout: 3500 })
            : window.setTimeout(run, 1800);

        window.addEventListener('load', idleRun, { once: true });
        ['pointerdown', 'keydown', 'scroll', 'touchstart'].forEach((eventName) => {
            window.addEventListener(eventName, run, { once: true, passive: true });
        });
    };

    scheduleThirdParty();

    const consentBox = document.getElementById('cookie-consent');
    if (consentBox && !localStorage.getItem('dk_cookie_consent')) {
        consentBox.classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    document.querySelectorAll('[data-cookie-action]').forEach((button) => {
        button.addEventListener('click', () => {
            localStorage.setItem('dk_cookie_consent', button.dataset.cookieAction);
            consentBox?.classList.add('hidden');
        });
    });

    const mobilePopupAd = document.querySelector('.mobile-popup-ad');
    const mobilePopupClose = document.querySelector('[data-mobile-popup-close]');
    const mobilePopupDismissKey = 'dk_mobile_popup_ad_dismissed';

    if (window.matchMedia('(max-width: 768px)').matches && mobilePopupAd?.querySelector('aside') && !sessionStorage.getItem(mobilePopupDismissKey)) {
        window.setTimeout(() => {
            mobilePopupAd.classList.add('is-open');
            mobilePopupAd.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
        }, 1200);
    }

    mobilePopupClose?.addEventListener('click', () => {
        mobilePopupAd?.classList.remove('is-open');
        mobilePopupAd?.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
        sessionStorage.setItem(mobilePopupDismissKey, '1');
    });

})();
</script>

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
    const button = document.getElementById("dkThemeToggle");
    if (!button) return;
    const applyTheme = (theme, persist = false) => {
        const next = theme === "dark" ? "dark" : "light";
        document.documentElement.dataset.theme = next;
        const action = next === "dark" ? "Gündüz moduna geç" : "Gece moduna geç";
        button.setAttribute("aria-label", action);
        button.setAttribute("title", action);
        const themeColor = document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]');
        if (themeColor) themeColor.setAttribute("content", next === "dark" ? "#17181b" : "#ffffff");
        document.querySelectorAll("[data-theme-logo]").forEach((logo) => {
            const source = next === "dark" ? logo.dataset.darkSrc : logo.dataset.lightSrc;
            if (source && logo.getAttribute("src") !== source) logo.setAttribute("src", source);
        });
        document.querySelectorAll(".full-mobile").forEach((section) => {
            if (!section.hasAttribute("data-theme-bg-saved")) {
                section.dataset.themeBg = section.style.getPropertyValue("background");
                section.dataset.themeBgPriority = section.style.getPropertyPriority("background");
                section.setAttribute("data-theme-bg-saved", "true");
            }
            if (next === "dark") {
                section.style.setProperty("background", "#1b1c20", "important");
            } else if (section.dataset.themeBg) {
                section.style.setProperty("background", section.dataset.themeBg, section.dataset.themeBgPriority || "");
            }
        });
        if (persist) {
            try { localStorage.setItem("dk-theme", next); } catch (_) {}
        }
    };
    applyTheme(document.documentElement.dataset.theme || "light");
    button.addEventListener("click", () => {
        applyTheme(document.documentElement.dataset.theme === "dark" ? "light" : "dark", true);
    });
});
</script>

<script>
(()=>{
    const labels=['Haber Gönder','Haber Ara','Son Dakika','Son Depremler','Hava Durumu','Piyasalar','Nöbetçi Eczaneler','Etkinlik Takvimi','İlçe Haberleri','Samsunspor Merkezi','Google Haberler’de Takip Et','RSS Servisleri'];
    const breaks=new Set(['Haber Ara','Son Dakika','Samsunspor Merkezi']);
    const normalize=value=>(value||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
    const sortServices=(root,dividerClass)=>{
        if(!root)return;
        root.querySelectorAll('.dk-service-divider,.footer-service-divider,.article-service-divider,.breaking-service-divider,.article-service-gap,.breaking-service-gap,.dk-menu-spacer').forEach(node=>node.remove());
        const children=[...root.children];
        labels.forEach(label=>{
            const item=children.find(node=>normalize(node.textContent).includes(label));
            if(!item)return;
            root.appendChild(item);
            if(breaks.has(label)){
                const divider=document.createElement(root.tagName==='UL'?'li':'span');
                divider.className=dividerClass;
                divider.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
                root.appendChild(divider);
            }
        });
    };
    const serviceTitle=[...document.querySelectorAll('.dk-menu-title')].find(node=>normalize(node.textContent)==='Servisler');
    sortServices(serviceTitle?.nextElementSibling,'dk-service-divider');
    const footerTitle=[...document.querySelectorAll('footer h4')].find(node=>normalize(node.textContent)==='Servisler');
    sortServices(footerTitle?.nextElementSibling,'footer-service-divider');
    document.querySelectorAll('.article-services-grid').forEach(root=>sortServices(root,'article-service-divider'));
    document.querySelectorAll('.breaking-services-grid').forEach(root=>sortServices(root,'breaking-service-divider'));
})();
</script>
